FOREWORD 

The preparation and prosecution of an application for patent to the end that the issued patent will adequately protect the particular invention are undertakings which require knowledge of patent law and Patent Office practice, as well as knowledge of the technical aspects of the invention. For this reason, most inventors engage registered patent attorneys or agents to do the work for them. 

The Patent Office maintains registers of attorneys and agents who have demonstrated their qualifications and are recognized to practice before the Office. Persons who are not recognized by the Patent Office for this practice are not permitted by law to represent inventors. Those registered as 'patent attorneys' are attorneys-at-law, except for some nonlawyers who were registered prior to 1938. Qualified persons who are not attorneys-at-law may be registered as patent agents. In the listings, a dagger (‡) appears beside the name of each registrant who is registered as a patent agent. Firms of patent attorneys and patent agents may also be registered.

In addition to the present publication, the Patent Office publishes from time to time an alphabetically arranged list of all persons and firms registered to practice entitled Roster of Attorneys and Agents Registered to Practice Before the U.S. Patent Office, available from the Superintendent of Documents, U.S. 

Government Printing Office, Washington, D.C., 20402 at $1.00 a copy. 

Registrants who are officers or employees of the U.S. Government cannot, otherwise than in the discharge of their official duties, represent inventors before the Patent Office (18 U.S.C. 203, 205). Further, some registrants have indicated that, because of their employment by corporations or for other reasons, they are not available to accept new clients. An asterisk (*) appears in the listing beside the name of each registrant who is not available to accept new clients. The listing for any corporation employee includes the name of the corporation.

The listing is arranged alphabetically by States and cities within States where the registrants have offices. Firms are listed following individuals. Registrants having offices elsewhere than in the United 

States are listed in a separate section, with countries and cities arranged alphabetically.
